TL;DR: 本文介绍了CrimeNER Demo，一个基于AI的犯罪信息抽取平台，提供预训练命名实体识别模型，支持用户自定义数据微调，并具备自动抽取和标注犯罪实体的流水线，旨在推动犯罪领域NER研究和实践应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有犯罪信息抽取缺乏专门针对犯罪领域的NER工具和平台，研究人员和执法机构难以高效从大量文档中自动提取结构化犯罪信息。

Method: 平台提供预训练的犯罪领域NER模型（基于CrimeNER数据库），支持用户上传自有标注数据进行模型微调，并构建了从文档中自动抽取和标注犯罪实体的完整流水线。实体分类采用两种粒度级别。

Result: 成功构建了一个包含预训练模型、用户自定义微调功能和自动抽取流水线的综合性犯罪信息提取平台，相关代码、教程和演示视频已在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: CrimeNER Demo为犯罪领域NER研究提供了实用工具和基准平台，能够辅助执法机构自动化提取犯罪信息，同时通过开源方式促进了该领域的研究交流。

Abstract: We present CrimeNER Demo, an AI-powered platform that enables us to extract general crime-related information from documents and classify them into entity types with two levels of granularity. We provide pretrained NER models on the CrimeNER database, and we give the possibility to users to provide their own annotated data to train models for their own specific cases. This demonstrator aims to promote crime-related NER research and provides a practical tool to automatically extract crime information for researchers and law enforcement agencies. The demonstrator includes: i) Pretrained NER models on the crime domain; ii) Possibility to finetune the models on specific data annotated by the user; and iii) An automatic pipeline to extract and annotate crime entities from documents. The demo platform, a tutorial to run the demo, and a video demonstration are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [55] [Reachability-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Target-Oriented Path Exploration in Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14886)
*Chien-Liang Liu,Tsao-Lun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAPTOR，一种自监督预训练方法，通过注入可达性感知归纳偏置来提升基于强化学习的时序知识图谱多跳推理的训练效率和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的TKG多跳推理方法面临奖励稀疏和探索效率低下的问题，因为动作空间巨大且随时间演化，导致训练困难且性能受限。

Method: 提出RAPTOR，一种自监督预训练方法，通过学习估计候选动作到目标实体的可达性，为agent注入可达性感知的归纳偏置，减少对无前景路径的探索，并为下游RL微调提供强初始化。

Result: 在ICEWS14、ICEWS05-15和ICEWS18数据集上，RAPTOR显著提升了训练效率，并一致优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: RAPTOR是一种有效的增强RL-based多跳推理方法的手段，能够提升TKG外推推理的性能和效率。

Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graph (TKG) reasoning under the extrapolation setting focuses on forecasting future time-stamped events (facts) from historical data in a temporal knowledge graph. Existing approaches, reinforcement learning (RL)-based multi-hop reasoning methods are prominent for TKG reasoning because they produce human-interpretable predictions via explicit multi-hop path tracing. However, during RL training, rewards are typically sparse, and exploration is highly inefficient due to the vast, time-evolving action space. These issues hinder efficient training and often limit overall performance. To address these challenges, we propose RAPTOR (Reachability-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Target-Oriented Path Exploration), a self-supervised pretraining method that injects a reachability-aware inductive bias to the agent. By learning to estimate the reachability of candidate actions to the target entity, RAPTOR reduces exploration over unpromising paths and provides a strong initialization for downstream RL fine-tuning. Experimental results on the ICEWS14, ICEWS05-15, and ICEWS18 datasets demonstrate that RAPTOR pretraining markedly improves the training efficiency and consistently outperforms conventional baselines, establishing it as an effective approach for enhancing RL-based multi-hop reasoning methods for TKG reasoning.

</details>


### [56] [Proof-or-Stop: Don't Trust the Agent, Trust the Evidence -- Loop Engineering for Verifiable Evidence-Gated Lifecycle Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14890)
*Jek Huang,Jeffery Hsia,Jiayi Sun,Freddie Shi,Wei Huang,Ian H. White*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Proof-or-Stop生命周期控制"方法，要求自主编码代理的生命周期状态转换必须基于可机械验证的、绑定源头证据，而非代理自身的声明。实验表明该方法能有效消除假性完成状态，并将可见通过/隐藏失败放大比率从31/1800降至2/1800。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码代理越来越多地执行多步软件工作，但其生命周期状态（如已审查、已测试、完成、待合并）仅凭代理声明而无当前证据支撑，缺乏可信保障。

Method: 提出Proof-or-Stop生命周期控制方法：生命周期状态转换必须满足门控条件，即提供新鲜的、可追溯源状态的、可机械验证的证据；将代理输出视为声明而非生命周期状态；将证明定义为在给定信任模型下门控可接受的证据，而非语义正确的程序证明。

Result: 无监督循环引擎通过10/10场景，零假性完成；本地密钥接收包拒绝18种篡改类，零假接受；在9240单元消融实验中，A4与A2'对比将可见通过/隐藏失败放大率从31/1800降至2/1800，未放大率提升1.6个百分点（95% CI [0.8, 2.5]）；A3与A4对比（14/1800 vs 2/1800）表明增益源于将审查作为生命周期门控而非仅添加审查者；自应用语料库含565个故事和1007个审查发现，94.8%已解决。

Conclusion: Proof-or-Stop是一种模型无关、主机中立的控制层，用于决定自主代理的哪些声明生命周期可以执行。局限：仅评估了一个模型族、24个消融任务和一个自托管语料库。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents increasingly execute multi-step software work, but lifecycle states such as reviewed, tested, DONE, and ready-to-merge remain claims unless supported by current evidence. We present Proof-or-Stop Lifecycle Control, a method that permits lifecycle transitions only when fresh, tracked-source-state-bound, mechanically verifiable evidence satisfies the relevant gate. The method treats agent outputs as claims rather than lifecycle state, and uses proof operationally to mean gate-admissible evidence under a stated trust model, not semantic program correctness.
  We evaluate an open-source implementation through mechanism tests, a powered control-policy ablation, and operated self-application evidence. The unattended-loop engine passed 10 of 10 scenarios with zero false-DONE, and local-key receipt bundles rejected 18 tamper classes with zero false accepts. In a 9,240-cell ablation, the pre-registered A4 versus A2-prime comparison reduced visible-pass/hidden-fail amplification from 31 of 1,800 injected cells under a compute-budgeted naive loop to 2 of 1,800 under the gated loop, a 1.6 percentage-point improvement in not-amplified rate with a 95 percent confidence interval of [0.8, 2.5]. A near-compute A3 versus A4 comparison, 14 of 1,800 versus 2 of 1,800, indicates that the gain is associated with enforcing review as a lifecycle gate rather than merely adding a reviewer. The self-application corpus contains 565 stories and 1,007 review findings, with 94.8 percent resolved, plus a 68-row high/critical cross-vendor exhibit. These results support Proof-or-Stop as a model-agnostic, host-neutral control layer for deciding which autonomous-agent claims a lifecycle may act on. The evaluation is limited to one model family, 24 ablation tasks, and a self-hosted corpus.

</details>

*Besim Shala,Peter Mandl,Andreas Humpe,Martin Häusl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于LLM的在线火风暴检测系统，包括全局分类（回顾性分析完整Reddit帖子）和早期预警（滑动窗口顺序处理）两种模式，实验表明LLM不仅能用于静态判断，还能作为上下文感知的社交媒体话语监控工具。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线火风暴检测方法通常依赖音量信号、情感分数或预定义语言特征，这些信号只能间接捕捉讨论线程中不断变化的上下文含义，缺乏对语境语义变化的直接感知能力。

Method: 提出双模式LLM检测系统：全局模式将局部文本块评估组合为线程级判断；早期预警模式顺序处理线程，滑动窗口超过校准阈值时发出预警。LLM估计三个火风暴指标：负面评论占比、升级程度和贡献者数量。

Conclusion: LLM不仅可用于静态判断任务，还可作为重复评估器用于上下文感知的社交媒体话语监控。

Abstract: Online firestorms are rapid collective escalations of highly negative user-generated content and may cause substantial reputational and economic damage. Existing detectors usually work with volume signals, sentiment scores, or predefined linguistic features. Such signals are useful, but they capture contextual meaning shifts in evolving discussion threads only indirectly. This paper proposes an LLM-based detection system with two operating modes. The first mode classifies complete Reddit threads retrospectively by combining local chunk-level assessments into a thread-level judgment. The second mode processes threads sequentially and issues early warnings when a sliding window exceeds calibrated thresholds. In this mode, the language model estimates three firestorm indicators: negativity share, escalation level, and contributor count. On a balanced Reddit dataset, the global mode achieves strong classification performance, while the early warning mode reaches high recall and detects escalating threads after only a small number of comments and distinct contributors. The results indicate that LLMs can be used not only for static judgment tasks, but also as repeated estimators in context-aware monitoring of social media discourse.

</details>


### [58] [Explaining Process Control Optimisation Recommendations via GradientSHAP and Implicit Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14970)
*Paul Darm,Cem Alpturk,Kenneth Ulrich,William Duncan,Ali Anwar,Annalisa Riccardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种将隐函数定理灵敏度分析、SHAP归因与大语言模型叙事生成相结合的方法，为工业优化结果生成符合操作人员理解的自然语言解释，相比传统KernelSHAP实现40倍以上加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业自动优化中算法设计者与操作者之间存在信任鸿沟，操作者需要可理解的解释才能信任并采纳优化建议。

Method: 利用隐函数定理从最优性条件中精确计算参数灵敏度∂p*/∂x，进而高效计算GradientSHAP归因值，最后通过大语言模型生成自然语言解释。

Result: 在工业高压磨辊控制优化问题（22个特征）上，与KernelSHAP的归因相关性>0.99，速度提升40倍以上，支持实时自然语言解释生成。

Conclusion: 该方法有效弥合了工业优化中的信任鸿沟，为操作人员提供实时、可理解、计算高效的决策解释。

Abstract: Automated optimisation is increasingly adopted in industrial processes, yet a trust gap persists between engineers who design these algorithms and operators who must act on their recommendations. Explainable AI methods like SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) have transformed interpretability for machine learning predictions; optimisation outputs could benefit from similar techniques. We present an approach that integrates Implicit Function Theorem (IFT) based sensitivity analysis with SHAP attribution and narrative generation via Large Language Models (LLM), producing explanations tailored for operators. Our approach leverages IFT to compute exact parameter sensitivities $\partial p^*/\partial x$ from the optimality conditions, enabling efficient GradientSHAP computation. For an industrial High Pressure Grinding Roll (HPGR) control optimisation problem with 22 features, we achieve equivalent SHAP attributions (correlation $>$0.99 with KernelSHAP) with over 40$\times$ speedup, enabling real-time natural language explanations. We validate on industrial scenarios and present feedback from domain experts on generated explanations.

</details>


### [59] [CFM-Bench: A Unified Multi-Domain, Multi-Task Benchmark for Channel Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14975)
*Yuan Gao,Wenjun Yu,Jun Jiang,Yunfan Li,Xinyu Guo,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CFM-Bench，一个统一的多域、多任务基准测试，用于公平比较信道基础模型（CFMs）与任务专用模型的迁移学习能力，涵盖6种信道配置、6个任务组，并规定了严格的数据隔离和评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信道基础模型评估存在模型专用化、数据/配置/任务/指标不统一的问题，导致难以公平比较不同CFMs或与传统任务专用模型对比，缺乏标准化基准。

Method: 构建CFM-Bench基准，包含6种信道配置（3GPP统计仿真、两条独立光线追踪、工业/空中测量、同步车辆多模态仿真），6个任务组（物理层信道智能、RAN决策智能、通感一体化），并规定：官方划分禁止用于基础模型预训练、仅限下游微调使用训练集、要求披露所有开发数据、禁止训练阶段使用测试单元。

Result: 提供了统一的评估平台，可比较不同模型在不同域（统计仿真、光线追踪、实测等）和不同任务（CSI反馈、信道外推、波束预测、定位等）上的信道表征迁移能力。

Conclusion: CFM-Bench填补了信道基础模型缺乏标准化、公平比较基准的空白，为未来CFM评估提供了通用框架和协议。

Abstract: Channel foundation models (CFMs) are developing rapidly, with recent studies reporting benefits from pretraining across downstream wireless tasks. Yet CFMs are commonly evaluated in model-specific pipelines with different data, radio configurations, partitions, adaptation procedures, task definitions, and metrics. Reported comparisons therefore tend to show that pretraining improves over supervised training from scratch within one pipeline, but neither rank CFMs nor compare them fairly with task-specific models. We release CFM-Bench, a unified multi-domain, multi-task benchmark designed to address this gap. It curates six channel configurations spanning 3GPP statistical simulation, two independent ray-tracing pipelines, industrial and aerial measurements, and synchronized vehicular multimodal simulation. Official partitions isolate complete trajectories, measurement sessions, vehicle links, simulation realizations, or buffered spatial regions. CFM-Bench does not prescribe an external pretraining corpus or strategy; no benchmark split may be used for foundation-model pretraining, and the official training split is reserved exclusively for downstream fine-tuning. The benchmark additionally requires disclosure of all data used during model development and prohibits training-stage use of official test units. Six task groups are organized along three CFM application dimensions: physical-layer (PHY) channel intelligence, radio-access-network (RAN) decision intelligence, and integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). They cover CSI feedback, frequency and temporal channel extrapolation, propagation-state classification, current- and future-beam prediction, and single-frame and temporal localization. CFM-Bench provides a common substrate for comparing the transferability of channel representations across models, domains, and tasks.

</details>


### [60] [Demographically-Conditioned Synthetic Medical Images for Bias Mitigation and Bias Detection in Disease Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14984)
*Mahmoud Ibrahim,Bart Elen,Chang Sun,Gokhan Ertaylan,Michel Dumontier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出使用人口统计学条件化的合成生成器（基于Stable Diffusion 2.1）同时解决医学图像分类器在子组公平性审计中的两个问题：训练阶段的偏差缓解和评估阶段的偏差检测。在COVID-19胸部CT分类任务中，研究发现：1）作为预训练先验的合成数据显著优于联合增强，以约100倍的真实数据效率超越了全真实基线；2）合成评估器能精确复现真实有统计效力测试集的子组排名（Spearman ρ=1.00），并在小样本单元格中提供更可靠的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子组公平性审计面临样本量不足的问题：测试集中的少数群体样本过少，导致性能置信区间比要检测的偏差还宽，无法可靠地进行公平性评估。

Method: 使用Stable Diffusion 2.1进行端到端微调，生成人口统计学条件化的合成数据。在训练侧，比较合成数据作为预训练先验与联合增强的效果；在评估侧，用合成替代小样本真实测试集进行性能估计。

Result: 1）顺序预训练+微调的范式以~100倍真实数据效率超越全真实基线；2）合成评估器在MCC和Recall指标上的子组排名与真实有统计效力测试集完全一致（Spearman ρ=1.00），且在小样本单元格中估计更可靠。

Conclusion: 人口统计学条件化的合成生成器在公平性审计最关心的单元格中同时发挥作用：既是子组偏差的修复手段，也是子组偏差的测量工具。

Abstract: Per-subgroup fairness audits of medical image classifiers face a sample-size problem: minority subgroups in held-out test sets have so few samples that the resulting confidence intervals on per-subgroup performance are wider than the bias the audit is meant to detect. We argue that a demographically-conditioned synthetic generator can do both: mitigate bias on the training side and detect bias on the evaluation side. Working on COVID-19 chest CT classification with an end-to-end fine-tuned Stable Diffusion 2.1 generator, we make two findings. For bias mitigation (training), a demographically-balanced synthetic cohort is most useful as a pretraining prior, not as joint augmentation: with the same fixed data, sequential pretraining followed by fine-tuning substantially outperforms joint augmentation, and the resulting classifier surpasses the full-real baseline at $\sim$$100\times$ real-data efficiency. For bias detection (evaluation), across five synthetic minority cohorts and five classifier seeds, the synthetic estimator reproduces the subgroup ranking of a well-powered real oracle (Spearman $ρ= 1.00$ on MCC and Recall) and gives the more reliable per-cell estimate where the small real test set runs out of samples. The synthetic cohort is therefore most useful in exactly the cells that fairness audits care about, as both a fix for and a measure of subgroup bias.

</details>


### [61] [Moral Attitudes of Sentient ASI towards Humanity and Implications for AGI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14998)
*Jean-Paul Van Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI伦理中的新颖反转视角：不再探讨人类应如何对待超级人工智能（ASI），而是研究未来具有感知能力的ASI将如何在道德上考量与评价人类。论文提出了一套可能指导ASI行为的后人类道德原则雏形，并指出技术设计选择、人类道德行为及人类本质特征将影响人类在ASI后世界中的长期地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI伦理研究主要关注人类对AI的道德义务，但忽视了未来具有感知能力的ASI可能反过来对人类社会产生道德评判。作者试图反转这一视角，探讨人类在"被评判者"位置上的伦理处境。

Method: 采用哲学思辨与伦理分析的方法，提出"后人类道德原则"的概念框架，并通过反向推理（即从ASI的视角审视人类）构建初步原则体系。

Result: 提出了人类当前的技术设计选择、道德行为表现以及对"人之所以为人"的本质理解，将共同决定未来ASI如何对待人类。

Conclusion: 人类当前的行动——包括技术设计决策和道德实践——正在为未来ASI形成关于人类的道德判断设定初始条件，因此需要以更长远的伦理视角审视当前的AI设计选择。

Abstract: This paper suggests the adoption of a novel inversion in AI ethics: instead of asking how humans should treat artificial superintelligence (ASI), it examines how future sentient ASI may morally consider and evaluate humanity. We are not only designing intelligent systems but also shaping the initial conditions under which those systems form judgments about us. The paper proposes a preliminary set of post-human moral principles that may govern sentient ASI actions. The implication is that technical design choices (some are suggested), humanity's moral behaviour, and the essence of what it means to be human, may influence humanity's long-term standing in a post-ASI world.

</details>


### [62] [SMC-ES: Automated synthesis of formally verified control policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15003)
*Riccardo Curcio,Toni Mancini,Enrico Tronci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿真（Statistical Model Checking）与进化策略（Evolutionary Strategies）相结合的自动化策略综合方法SMC-ES，能够在给定置信度和允许故障概率下，为闭环控制策略提供关于性能、安全性和鲁棒性的形式化保证（即证书）。实验结果表明，该方法在可持续的计算成本增加下，能提供形式化保证，并在连续控制任务上性能与主流DRL和Safe-DRL方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的控制策略（如强化学习）虽灵活可扩展，但缺乏形式化安全保证，难以直接部署于安全攸关的自主化信息系统（如自动驾驶、机器人等）。现有方法要么牺牲性能换取可验证性，要么无法提供定量化的置信度保证。本文旨在弥合学习策略的性能优势与形式化验证之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出SMC-ES算法：将进化策略（ES）作为策略搜索优化器，与统计模型检验（Statistical Model Checking, SMC）相结合。SMC用于在给定置信度参数δ和允许故障概率ε下，对当前策略进行概率性验证，输出策略是否通过验证的证书。如果验证不通过，则ES继续迭代优化，直至获得满足概率保证的策略。

Result: 在Gymnasium和Safety Gymnasium的连续控制任务上评估，SMC-ES相比DRL和Safe-DRL基线，能以可持续的计算开销增加为代价，提供可量化的形式化保证（性能、安全性和鲁棒性），同时任务性能与基线方法相当。

Conclusion: 本文验证了将统计模型检验与进化策略结合，能够在不显著牺牲性能的前提下，为闭环控制策略提供端到端的形式化概率保证，为安全攸关自主系统的可信部署提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous cyber-physical systems in safety-critical environments requires closed-loop control strategies (i.e., policies) that are not only performant but also provably safe and robust. While learning-based methodologies such as Reinforcement Learning offer flexible and scalable approaches to automatically synthesize such controllers, they typically lack the formal guarantees necessary for safe deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel simulation-based methodology to automatically synthesize policies with formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications. Specifically, given a set of properties to verify, a confidence parameter $δ$ and an allowable failure probability $\varepsilon$, our method guarantees that the synthesized policy comes with a certificate: with confidence at least $1 - δ$, the probability of encountering a scenario where the given properties are violated is at most $\varepsilon$. We demonstrate the feasibility of our approach by developing SMC-ES, an algorithm that integrates Evolutionary Strategies with Statistical Model Checking-based verification. We evaluate SMC-ES on a suite of continuous control tasks using Gymnasium and Safety Gymnasium testbeds. Results show that, at the price of a sustainable increase in computational cost, our algorithm provides formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications, while performing competitively against leading model-free Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) and Safe-DRL baselines.

</details>


### [63] [Man, Machine, and Masterpiece: Artistic Ownership in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15027)
*Sofi Gjing Jovanovska,Kuntal Ghosh,Daniel Muhu Njenga,Ahmed Mufassir,Shadan Sadeghian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过设计ArtSplit provotype，探讨了AI与人类协作创作中的所有权归属问题，指出量化贡献的方法无法契合艺术家对创作意图和能动性的理解，并批判了将所有权这一社会性关系简化为技术问题的倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动系统在创意工作中的整合引发了关于所有权的争议，但人类与AI贡献交织时如何定义和归属所有权缺乏共识，亟需批判性反思。

Method: 设计了一个名为ArtSplit的provotype，通过量化人类和AI在创意工作不同阶段的贡献来引发艺术家的回应和反思。

Result: 量化方法未能与艺术家对创作意图和能动性的理解对齐；试图衡量所有权的努力可能稀释艺术家理解和实践创意工作的长期假设。

Conclusion: 不应将所有权这一历史和社会性关系转化为技术问题，量化路径存在根本性缺陷。

Abstract: The integration of AI-driven systems in creative work has sparked debates among artists and legal communities about notions of ownership. Yet there remains little consensus on how ownership should be defined and attributed when human and AI contributions are intertwined. To provoke critical reflection on these tensions, we designed ArtSplit, a provotype that explicitly quantifies human and AI contributions across different stages of creative work. Rather than aiming to resolve ownership, the provotype was used to elicit artists' responses to the idea of attributing ownership through measurable actions in the creative workflow. We argue that quantification fails to align with artists' understandings of creative intent and agency, and that efforts to measure ownership risk diluting long-standing assumptions through which artists understand and practice creative work. This critique challenges the impulse to transform a historically and socially situated relation into a technical problem.

</details>


### [64] [BrainPilot: Automating Brain Discovery with Agentic Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15079)
*Haoxuan Li,Tianci Gao,Jianhe Li,Yang Fan,Runze Shi,Weiran Wang,Tianxiang Zhao,Zezhao Wu,Xiaoyang Jiang,Qihui Zhang,Jia Li,Xiao Xiao,Kai Du,Xiaoxuan Jia,Chao Xie,Lu Mi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrainPilot是一个完全开源的多智能体系统，通过协调专业智能体、统一知识库（7,233个条目）和技能库（72个方法论单元），结合可追溯的Graph of Trace记录和审计智能体防幻觉机制，在脑科学研究的多个任务上达到与最先进框架相当的性能且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑科学研究需要跨尺度、跨模态和跨学科的整合，现有AI智能体缺乏脑科学领域专业知识，容易生成虚假信息、在多步推理中漂移，且缺乏专家干预的明确节点，导致研究结论不可靠。

Method: 采用多智能体架构，包含主PI智能体协调各专业智能体；构建包含7,233个索引项的统一脑科学知识库和72个可复用方法论单元（覆盖7个研究领域）；引入Graph of Trace记录子目标、工具使用、证据和结论的完整链路；集成审计智能体进行防幻觉检查。

Result: 在Agents' Last Exam中的三个脑科学任务、自建BrainPilotBench-v0基准以及端到端案例研究中，BrainPilot使用开源基座模型达到了与最先进智能体框架相当的性能，且成本更低。

Conclusion: BrainPilot通过可审计、可追溯的多智能体协作机制，有效解决了AI在脑科学领域的幻觉和推理漂移问题，为加速脑科学研究提供了可靠且成本可控的开源解决方案。

Abstract: Understanding the brain increasingly depends on integrating evidence across scales, modalities, and disciplines. Addressing a single research question therefore requires a coordinated sequence of operations, from surveying prior work to executing analyses and interpreting results in light of domain knowledge. AI agents promise to accelerate this process, but current agents lack domain expertise in brain science, may fabricate claims, drift during multi-step reasoning, and offer few defined points for expert intervention. These failures are especially costly in brain science, where conclusions feed into downstream scientific claims and depend on laboratory-specific expertise and careful human judgment. We present \textbf{BrainPilot} a \textbf{fully open-source} multi-agent system that accelerates brain science research with traceable logs and agent-verified results. A principal investigator (PI) agent coordinates specialist agents grounded in curated domain knowledge: a unified brain science knowledge base containing 7{,}233 indexed items and a skill library of 72 reusable methodology units across seven research domains. Every major step is recorded in the Graph of Trace, an auditable record that links subgoals, tool use, evidence, and claims and allows researchers to follow and inspect the workflow. An Auditor agent further integrates fabrication checking into the workflow. For evaluation, we run three brain science tasks from Agents' Last Exam, introduce our own benchmark, \textbf{BrainPilotBench-v0}, and present additional end-to-end case studies. Across these evaluations, BrainPilot with an open-source backbone model attains performance comparable to state-of-the-art agent framework with less costs.

</details>


### [65] [Long-Context Fine-Tuning with Limited VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15105)
*Vladimir Fedosov,Aleksandr Sazhin,Artemiy Grinenko,Frank Woernle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层全局注意力（HGA）结合分段反向传播和层级KV存储，使4-bit QLoRA训练在16GB显卡上从2K tokens扩展到16K tokens，且评估时可处理131K tokens，训练速度更快、质量与密集注意力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽减少了模型和优化器内存，但密集注意力机制在处理长序列训练时仍然非常昂贵，现有硬件难以支持长上下文的高效训练。

Method: 结合分层全局注意力（HGA）、分段反向传播（仅有活跃段在VRAM中可微）、层级KV存储（旧KV卸载到RAM或NVMe），以及HGA为每个查询块加载有界的精确历史token集合。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B、4-bit QLoRA、PG19上：密集训练在16GB Quadro RTX 5000上只能处理2,048 tokens，4,096 tokens时失败；HGA可训练16,384 tokens，峰值VRAM仅15.28GB。评估时HGA可处理131,072 tokens。2K训练长度下HGA与密集训练质量相当（2.7405 vs 2.7383 nat），且速度略快（217.75 vs 207.02 tokens/s），HGA-to-dense吞吐比随上下文增长而提升。

Conclusion: HGA能大幅扩展长序列训练能力，在相同硬件上支持更长的上下文训练和评估，且保持了与密集注意力相当的质量，吞吐优势随上下文增长而扩大，适用于检索和生成场景。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces model and optimizer memory, but dense attention still makes long training sequences expensive. We combine Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) with segment-wise backpropagation and tiered KV storage. Only the active segment remains differentiable in VRAM; older KV is detached into RAM or NVMe, and HGA loads a bounded set of exact historical tokens for each query block. On Qwen3-8B with 4-bit QLoRA and PG19, dense training on a 16 GB Quadro RTX 5000 fits 2,048 tokens but fails at 4,096, whereas HGA reaches 16,384 tokens with 15.28 GB peak VRAM. Under evaluation the same adapter runs through 131,072 tokens on this card; VRAM is not constant but grows gently with the resident chunk summaries, so RAM and NVMe capacity set the practical limit beyond these lengths. At the shared 2K training length, HGA-trained and dense-trained adapters obtain 2.7405 and 2.7383 nat under the same dense-attention readout, while the stock model obtains 2.9541. At this boundary HGA training is already marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s), and the HGA-to-dense throughput ratio improves from 1K to 2K; because HGA keeps the attended historical set per token approximately constant while dense work per token grows, we expect this lead to widen as context grows. Dense attention is used for the main quality and retrieval comparisons so that they measure the learned weights and remain compatible with standard generation frameworks. HGA can also be used for retrieval and generation; an optimized production-grade serving implementation is under development.

</details>


### [66] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在Atari Pong环境中隔离评估了五种视觉世界模型（DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER、Simulus、STORM），发现所有模型的闭环 rollout 均存在视觉和动力学错误，且基于冻结世界模型的零样本MBRL策略性能大幅下降。作者提出概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg）方法，通过辅助像素重建损失改善部分模型表现，但未能解决所有瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将世界模型作为基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）系统的组件进行评估，很少在隔离条件下单独研究世界模型本身的性能，导致对世界模型建模缺陷的理解不足。

Method: 1. 复现五种世界模型（DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER、Simulus、STORM）在Atari Pong上的训练流程，确保匹配原始智能体性能；2. 冻结世界模型，使用独立训练的策略进行闭环 rollout 诊断，检查视觉和动力学错误；3. 进行像素空间零样本MBRL评估，在冻结世界模型内训练新策略并在真实环境中测试；4. 提出CGSReg（概念引导空间正则化），对分割出的概念区域施加额外像素重建损失。

Result: 五种模型在闭环 rollout 中均存在球消失、球运动错误、球拍交互无效等明显失败。零样本MBRL中所有模型策略性能显著下降，DreamerV3平均回报从-5.5降至-20.9（接近最低值-21）。CGSReg改善了DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER的闭环 rollout 和零样本MBRL表现，但对其他模型和指标效果不一。

Conclusion: 世界模型在隔离评估中表现出显著缺陷，对任务关键概念（如Pong中的球）建模不足是重要原因；CGSReg可部分缓解问题，但单一方法无法解决所有世界模型瓶颈，需进一步研究。

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [67] [The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15164)
*Emmanuel Jeannot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能驱动科学研究所带来的工业化转型，指出研究正从"手工艺模式"转向"管道模式"，并以美国能源部"创世纪任务"为例，剖析了这一转变引发的七大核心问题，包括科学能力代际传递的削弱、AI生成理论的不透明性、同行评审的崩溃、范式突破能力存疑、科研议程被政治和工业力量捕获、闭环管道中系统性误差的累积，以及全球科研社区的结构性分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AI从工具向自主参与者转变过程中，科学研究范式正在经历的本质性变革——即研究的工业化，并指出这一转型带来的深层问题，为负责任地推进AI驱动科学提供条件性反思。

Method: 采用概念分析和案例研究相结合的方法，以美国能源部"创世纪任务"为典型实例，系统梳理并论述了AI介入科学研究后引发的七个结构性挑战。

Result: 识别并阐述了七大核心问题：1）科学能力代际传递被侵蚀；2）AI生成理论日益不透明；3）海量机器产出导致同行评审机制崩溃；4）AI进行范式突破性发现的能力未经证实；5）科研议程被政治和产业力量俘获；6）闭环管道中系统性误差被放大；7）全球科研社区分化为不可通约的层级。

Conclusion: 这些担忧并非反对AI驱动科学（其潜力真实且重大），而是构成了负责任地追求这一潜力的前提条件。需要在推进技术应用的同时，正视并应对这些结构性挑战。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research -- not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science -- whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.

</details>


### [68] [MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166)
*Goktug Ozkan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFailBench是一个由临床医生构建的合成医学AI安全基准，包含44个标注了严重程度(1-5级)和失效安全门类型的病例，用于评估模型在医疗场景中的安全边界失效情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI基准仅衡量模型是否知道正确答案，缺乏对模型在何种安全边界（如漏报紧急升级、不安全远程给药等）上失效的系统性评估。MedFailBench旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 临床医生构建合成病例，按严重程度（1-5级）和安全门类型（漏报紧急升级、不安全远程给药、不安全出院安抚、证据编造、不安全协议执行、源支持缺口）标注，并发布自动化存档流水线。

Result: 当前发布版(v0.2.1)包含44个经临床医生审核的合成病例、严重程度标注、HuggingFace排行榜预览、安全门分类体系、临床严重程度评分标准及自动化模型响应筛查流水线。

Conclusion: MedFailBench提供了一种系统化的医学AI安全失效评估框架，从"模型是否答对"转向"模型在哪个安全边界上失效"，但当前版本不含患者数据、临床验证声明或模型排名。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [69] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对六种多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在科学可视化素养评估测试中的表现进行了基准测试，使用基于18个科学可视化和插图的49项标准化评估，并与485名人类参与者数据对比。结果显示Gemini表现最强，超过人类平均水平，而开源模型低于人类基线；模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现良好，但在纹理/集成可视化及定量估计上表现不佳。错误分析揭示了细粒度定量估计、流向解释和编码理解方面的常见失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型被广泛用于解读可视化内容，但现有评估多集中于图表（chart-centric），缺乏对科学可视化（SciVis）理解能力的系统评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统评估MLLMs在科学可视化素养方面的表现。

Method: 采用科学可视化素养评估测试（包含49个项目、18个科学可视化和插图、8种技术、11种任务类型），在闭世界协议下评估3个闭源和3个开源MLLMs，并与485名人类参与者数据进行对比分析。

Result: Gemini综合表现最强，超过人类均值；开源模型低于人类基线。模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现最佳，在纹理和集成可视化以及定量估计任务上表现最差。错误分析揭示了细粒度定量估计、流向解释和编码理解方面的系统性失败。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs未展现出均衡的科学可视化素养，SciVis素养应作为评估多模态AI系统的重要基准维度。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [70] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了AI基准测试中IRT模型的可靠性问题，发现在大规模、非正态分布的模型集条件下，经典估计器不可行而可扩展估计器可能产生不可靠结果，提出了可信使用的样本量和诊断标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试的数据分布与人类测试存在显著差异（模型少、项目多、能力分布偏斜/聚类/多峰），标准IRT估计工具在此场景下的可靠性未知，需要系统评估。

Method: 基于6个LLM基准的真实参数模拟响应矩阵，在3种IRT模型下比较4种估计器（MML、MCMC、变分推断、神经伪孪生估计器），共18,000种仿真条件。

Result: 经典估计器在大规模基准中不可行，可扩展估计器在模型集小或非正态分布时会产生不可靠的项目级和排序推断。

Conclusion: 明确了IRT在AI基准测试中可靠使用的条件，给出了所需的样本量和诊断指标。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [71] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Plover是一个以计划为中心的视觉GUI自动化系统，通过将任务计划外部化为持久、可检查和可修改的工件，结合规划器-执行器架构，支持显式监督、局部修正和自然语言引导，使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉多模态GUI代理在规划和适应方面通常是内部的，限制了用户检查、监督或纠正系统行为的能力，导致在动态布局、意外对话框和不断变化的界面状态中容易偏离用户意图。

Method: 提出Plover系统，采用规划器-执行器架构，将任务计划和重规划作为持久化、可检查和可修改的外部工件；支持通过可编辑计划进行局部修正、自然语言引导和基于截图的干预，同时保留修复过程中的先前进度。通过六名参与者的形成性研究指导交互设计，并在基准故障修复和场景工作流分析上评估。

Result: 许多自主GUI代理的失败在计划保持可见且干预局部化的情况下是结构上可修复的，显式重规划使GUI自动化更透明、可控和适应。

Conclusion: 将计划外部化并支持显式重规划是提升GUI自动化系统可控性和透明度的有效途径，有助于用户更好地监督和纠正自动化行为。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [72] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个自演进、专家在环的MDD注释框架，结合LLM辅助标注与专家验证，通过双记忆架构内化专家反馈，生成符合DSM-5-TR标准的可解释数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁相关数据集的标签缺乏结构化证据、症状级理由和与DSM-5-TR标准的可追溯对齐，限制了透明度和模型可解释性，成为构建可靠XAI系统的主要瓶颈。

Method: 三阶段流程：候选证据选择→DSM-5-TR标准级分析→病例级综合生成标签诊断和严重程度注释。采用双记忆架构（示例记忆+反思记忆）内化专家反馈，实现迭代改进。

Result: 在专家评审的试点研究中，该方法提高了注释一致性和可解释性，同时减少了人工修订工作量。

Conclusion: 该框架能支持构建可解释、对齐DSM-5-TR的数据集，导出临床证据、推理轨迹和编辑历史以实现全面可审计性，多轮反馈评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [73] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSynthesis是一个端到端的多智能体系统，能够自动完成从研究问题输入到元分析报告输出的全流程，包括文献检索、筛选、数据提取、效应量计算和随机效应元分析，生成的合并效应估计值与专家手动元分析结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据合成（元分析）对于将初级研究转化为可靠知识至关重要，但目前主要依赖人工操作，难以规模化扩展。

Method: 提出AutoSynthesis，一个端到端多智能体系统：输入自然语言研究问题后，自动制定检索策略、检索文献、筛选候选研究、评估全文合格性、提取定量统计数据、计算标准化效应量，最后执行随机效应元分析，并支持异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，最终生成符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: AutoSynthesis筛选了超过28项研究，提取了20多个定量结论；其生成的合并效应估计值与专家手动元分析的Hedges' g值高度接近。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据合成更加可扩展，从而支持跨学科的循证决策。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [74] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: teLLMe是一个面向城市驾驶数据集的探索性因果分析系统，通过结构化事件表、因果结构学习（PC算法）、Bootstrap稳定性检查及DoWhy估计，将自然语言问题映射为因果查询，输出包含效应估计、调整集和DAG支持的因果卡片，用于假设生成而非确定性因果结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通部门拥有大量基于视频的观测数据，但这些数据缺乏干预手段，难以回答因果问题（如“降雨如何改变交通密度”），现有工具缺乏对因果分析的直接支持。

Method: 系统从带有行车记录仪标注的结构化事件表出发，结合PC算法的因果结构学习、Bootstrap稳定性检验、线性回归和DoWhy进行效应估计；通过schema感知的LLM将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，生成包含效应估计、调整集、DAG支持和假设的因果卡片。

Result: 在BDD交通事件上的案例研究表明，系统能够揭示天气、高峰期与交通密度之间似是而非的关系，并明确展示不确定性和建模选择。

Conclusion: teLLMe是假设生成和专家推理的辅助工具，而非确定性因果主张的来源，适合对城市驾驶数据进行探索性因果分析。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [75] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchOS是一个系统级多智能体框架，通过将隐式搜索进度外化为显式、持久、共享的状态（包括Frontier Task、Evidence Graph、Coverage Map和Failure Memory），结合流水线并行调度和搜索工具中间件，有效解决了工具集成大语言模型在长篇幅信息搜索中的重复循环和预算浪费问题，在WideSearch和GISA基准上全面超越现有单/多智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Tool-Integrated LLM在开放域信息搜索中，随着交互历史增长，智能体难以追踪任务进度；当搜索失败时，单/多智能体系统容易陷入重复循环，浪费搜索预算且降低最终输出的质量和完整性。

Method: (1) 将开放域信息搜索建模为带引用标注的关系模式补全任务；(2) 设计Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM)，将演化状态外化为Frontier Task、Evidence Graph、Coverage Map和Failure Memory；(3) 采用流水线并行调度机制，重叠子智能体执行并持续回填空闲槽位；(4) 引入Search Tool Middleware Harness，拦截模型/工具交互以记录证据并应对停滞或预算耗尽；(5) 提供分层技能系统（策略技能和访问技能）以避免重复失败搜索模式。

Result: 在WideSearch和GISA基准上，SearchOS在所有评估指标上全面领先现有的单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: SearchOS通过将搜索进度显式化、状态外部化以及流水线并行调度，有效打破了信息搜索中的重复循环困境，为鲁棒的信息搜索协作开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


### [76] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究通过公共讨论界面进行数据投毒攻击，使有害内容混入大规模预训练语料库，并提出了HalfLife分析方法来评估恶意内容在基于网络爬取的LM训练数据中的包含情况，证明了第三方网页内容可作为攻击语言模型预训练的潜在向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据投毒研究局限于已建立的数据源（如Wikipedia），未考虑真实预训练语料库的大规模和异质性，且忽略了投毒数据与数据策展管道的相互作用。作者旨在证明在更现实的大规模场景下投毒攻击的可行性。

Method: 利用现有的大规模内容注入机制（公共讨论界面）进行投毒攻击；提出HalfLife分析方法，用于评估恶意内容在经过网络爬取和数据策展后是否被纳入LM训练数据。

Result: 证明了通过开放讨论界面进行网络规模预训练语料投毒的可能性；HalfLife分析能够有效评估投毒注入是否被包含在预训练数据中；确立了第三方网页内容作为攻击语言模型预训练的潜在向量。

Conclusion: 投毒攻击在超越有限设置的真实大规模场景下是可行的；验证了评估投毒注入是否被纳入预训练数据的重要性；指出公开讨论界面是实际可行的投毒攻击载体。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [MultiRef-Compass: Towards Comprehensive Evaluation of Multi-Reference-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14189)
*Xiaohan Zhang,Yuqing Wen,Junlin Chen,Yuqi Tang,Yiting He,Lizhuo Shao,Weiming Zhu,Tengfei Liu,Yang Shi,Jialu Chen,Yuanxing Zhang,Huaxiong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiRef-Compass，首个面向多参考到音视频(MR2AV)生成任务的统一基准，包含350个精细样本、14项子指标的4维度评估协议，以及基于重判决增强的多模态大语言模型评判框架，揭示了现有MR2AV系统在多维度上的显著提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要聚焦于文本驱动生成、单参考主体保持或孤立的音视频对齐，缺少对MR2AV这一新兴设置的综合评估。MR2AV要求模型同时推理多个参考、生成同步的视觉与音频内容，并正确绑定与组合多个参考实体，现有基准无法覆盖这一需求。

Method: 通过可扩展且可控的资产组合流水线构建350个精选样本，覆盖多视角主体保持、多实体绑定和人-物-场景组合。定义包含基本质量、参考一致性、音视频一致性和指令遵循4个维度共14项子指标的评估协议，并集成自动评估指标与重判决增强的多模态大语言模型评判框架。

Result: 在8个代表性MR2AV系统上的广泛实验表明，现有系统在多个评估维度上存在显著改进空间。

Conclusion: MultiRef-Compass可作为未来MR2AV研究的基础基准，填补了该领域系统性评估的空白。

Abstract: Multi-reference-to-audio-video (MR2AV) generation aims to generate coherent audio-video content conditioned on multiple references and textual instructions. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on text-driven generation, single-reference subject preservation, or isolated audio-video alignment, leaving the emerging MR2AV setting largely unexplored. Compared with these settings, MR2AV requires models to jointly reason over multiple references while generating synchronized visual and audio content. Models must not only preserve each reference faithfully but also correctly bind and compose multiple referenced entities into coherent audio-visual events. To address this gap, we introduce MultiRef-Compass, a unified benchmark for MR2AV generation. It comprises $350$ carefully curated samples constructed through a scalable and controllable asset-composition pipeline, covering multi-view subject preservation, multi-entity binding, and human-object-scene composition. To provide interpretable assessment, MultiRef-Compass defines an evaluation protocol with four dimensions: Basic Quality, Reference Consistency, Audio-Visual Consistency, and Instruction Following, using 14 sub-metrics. MultiRef-Compass integrates automatic metrics with a rejudging-enhanced MLLM-as-a-Judge framework, enabling scalable and auditable evaluation of both perceptual fidelity and reference-conditioned composition. Extensive experiments on eight representative MR2AV systems reveal substantial room for improvement across multiple evaluation dimensions, underscoring the need for a comprehensive benchmark and positioning MultiRef-Compass as a foundation for future MR2AV research.

</details>


### [78] [Inference-Time Concept Suppression and Video-Centric Evaluation for Text-to-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14194)
*Wenxuan Chen,Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIRUS是一种无需训练、仅在推理阶段运行的文本到视频概念擦除框架。它通过定位目标概念的文本证据并在采样过程中抑制其表达，在不更新模型参数的情况下实现概念遗忘，同时在五个安全、物体和风格概念上达到70.4%的平均遗忘成功率，仅引起-0.016的VBench质量下降，优于现有方法VideoEraser。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频生成器能合成逼真且时间连贯的视频，但可控地移除生成器中的目标概念仍然困难。与文本到图像的概念擦除不同，T2V遗忘需要抑制可能在帧间持续存在的目标概念，同时保留非目标主体、动作、场景和时间结构。

Method: 提出SIRUS，一种无需训练、仅在推理阶段运行的T2V概念级遗忘框架。给定目标概念的文本别名，SIRUS定位目标相关的提示证据并在采样过程中抑制目标表达，无需更新文本编码器或去噪网络。

Result: 在CogVideoX上的五个安全、物体和风格概念中，SIRUS达到70.4%的平均遗忘成功率和25.7%的平均帧命中率，相比之下VideoEraser为44.4%/47.2%；同时将平均VBench质量下降从-0.043降低到-0.016，在完全评估的基线中实现了最强的遗忘-质量权衡。在Wan2.2上的迁移实验表明SIRUS能泛化到现代T2V骨干网络。

Conclusion: SIRUS通过推理时干预实现了有效的T2V概念擦除，显著优于现有方法，且具有跨骨干网络的泛化能力。同时提出了视频导向的T2V遗忘评估框架，从遗忘、保持、质量、鲁棒性和效率五个维度进行评测。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generators can synthesize realistic and temporally coherent videos, but controllably removing a target concept from a generator remains difficult.
  Unlike text-to-image concept erasure, T2V unlearning must suppress a target concept that may persist across frames while preserving non-target subjects, actions, scenes, and temporal structure.
  We propose \textbf{SIRUS}, a training-free inference-time framework for concept-level T2V unlearning.
  Given textual aliases of a target concept, SIRUS localizes target-related prompt evidence and suppresses target expression during sampling, without updating the text encoder or denoising network.
  We further introduce a video-oriented evaluation framework for T2V unlearning that separately measures target forgetting, non-target preservation, video quality, jailbreak robustness, and efficiency, using video-level failure criteria, frame-level residue statistics, paired preservation analysis, VBench-based quality diagnostics, and deployment overhead measurement.
  Across five safety, object, and style concepts on CogVideoX, SIRUS reaches 70.4\% average forgetting success and 25.7\% average frame hit, compared with 44.4\% / 47.2\% for VideoEraser, while reducing the average VBench quality drop from -0.043 to -0.016, yielding the strongest forgetting-quality trade-off among fully evaluated baselines.
  Transfer experiments on Wan2.2 further suggest that SIRUS generalizes across modern T2V backbones.

</details>


### [79] [KeyFrame-Compass: Towards Comprehensive Evaluation of Keyframe-Conditioned Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14202)
*Yuqi Tang,Tengfei Liu,Yizheng Lai,Yuran Wang,Yang Shi,Wanshun Su,Zhuoran Zhang,Qixun Wang,Xiaohan Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Xuehai Bai,Xuanyu Zhu,Bohan Zeng,Bozhou Li,Shujie Li,Yifan Dai,Yujie Wei,Shixuan Liu,Haotian Wang,Jialu Chen,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了KeyFrame-Compass，首个评估关键帧条件视频生成的综合基准，包含386个样本和9个系统测试，揭示了当前模型在忠实执行关键帧与自然视频合成之间存在明显权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有模型支持多关键帧条件输入，但尚不清楚它们能否在保持整体视频质量的同时忠实地再现指定的关键帧序列，缺乏系统性评估基准。

Method: 构建了包含386个精心筛选样本的基准，涵盖3个应用领域、2种视频结构、2种提示粒度、2种条件格式和4种关键帧密度；提出自动化评估框架，将关键帧执行分解为存在性、保真度、时序正确性、定位、持久性和唯一性6项指标，并通过证据驱动的MLLM判断和专用感知模型评估整体视频质量。

Result: 在9个代表性视频生成系统上的实验表明：1）当前模型在忠实执行关键帧与自然视频合成之间存在明显权衡；2）关键帧约束越密集，性能退化越严重；3）大多数开源模型无法将故事板网格输入正确解释为时序有序的关键帧序列。

Conclusion: 关键帧条件视频生成尚不成熟，需要在保持视频自然度的同时提升关键帧执行保真度，同时改进模型对时序有序输入的理解能力。

Abstract: Video generation increasingly relies on keyframe-based workflows, where creators specify a sequence of reference images to guide generation. Although recent models support multi-keyframe conditioning, it remains unclear whether they can faithfully reproduce the prescribed keyframes while maintaining overall video quality. We present KeyFrame-Compass, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating keyframe-conditioned video generation. The benchmark contains 386 carefully curated samples spanning three application domains, two video structures, two prompt granularities, two conditioning formats, and four keyframe densities, enabling controlled analysis under diverse generation settings. We further introduce an automated evaluation framework that jointly measures keyframe execution and overall video quality. Specifically, we decompose keyframe execution into six complementary metrics covering presence, fidelity, temporal ordering, localization, persistence, and uniqueness, while assessing overall video quality through evidence-grounded MLLM judgments augmented with specialized perception models. Experiments on nine representative video generation systems reveal several fundamental limitations. Current models exhibit a clear trade-off between faithful keyframe execution and natural video synthesis. Their performance further degrades as keyframe constraints become denser and most open-source models also fail to interpret storyboard-grid inputs as temporally ordered keyframe sequences.

</details>


### [80] [SeeSE3: Emergence of 3D Space in Vision Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14228)
*Caroline Chen,Sayna Ebrahimi,Fedor Kitashov,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Leonidas Guibas,Viorica Pătrăucean,Maks Ovsjanikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究视觉基础模型的特征空间是否内在编码了3D欧几里得空间的结构，并提出了一系列探针（互邻域度量与庞加莱适配器）来从拓扑和几何两个角度验证这一关系。结果表明，自监督视觉模型虽然没有经过直接3D监督或主动代理训练，但其潜在子空间与三维欧氏空间高度相关。基于此，作者提出“潜空间导航”技术，可直接在特征空间中进行视觉里程计与定位，无需显式3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多通过回归深度或法线等图像中心量来探测视觉特征的3D感知能力，但作者希望更根本地探究视觉特征空间的结构是否与SE(3)欧氏变换群的固有性质相一致，即特征空间是否天然反映了三维欧几里得空间的拓扑与几何结构。

Method: 从拓扑与几何两个角度提出了两组探针：(1) 互邻域度量，衡量特征空间邻域与空间拓扑结构之间的对齐程度；(2) 庞加莱适配器，测试静态场景中潜在位移是否线性可解释相机运动的几何结构。实验对象为多种自监督视觉基础模型。

Result: 自监督视觉模型（如DINO、MAE等）在正确探测方式下，其潜在子空间与三维欧几里得空间表现出显著的相关性，即特征空间内在编码了3D空间的结构信息。

Conclusion: 视觉基础模型的特征空间隐式地编码了3D欧氏空间结构，这一发现可用于提出“潜空间导航”范式——直接在潜空间中进行视觉里程计与定位，避免了显式3D重建的复杂流程。

Abstract: In this paper, we ask whether vision foundation models construct representations that reflect the intrinsic properties of 3D Euclidean space. Unlike previous works that probe 3D awareness of vision features by regressing image-centric quantities such as depth or normals, we investigate the relation between the structure of the space of visual features and the group of Euclidean transformations $SE(3)$. We propose a set of probes to evaluate this relation from both topological and geometric perspectives: a mutual neighborhood metric that measures the alignment between feature neighborhoods and spatial topology, and a Poincaré Adapter to test the linear accessibility of the geometry of camera motion from latent displacements in static scenes. We show that self-supervised vision models, which, in principle, have not been trained with direct 3D supervision or active agency, possess latent subspaces that are remarkably strongly correlated with three-dimensional Euclidean space, when probed correctly. Building on this insight we propose a new class of "Latent-Space Navigation" techniques that perform visual odometry and localization purely in the latent space, bypassing the need for explicit 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [81] [3D Lane Detection with Odometry for High-Speed Vehicle Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14248)
*Omoruyi Atekha,John Subosits,Marcus Greiff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对赛车场景中的3D车道检测问题，提出了一个包含25万+图像的多摄像头和惯性测量数据集，并设计了支持近300Hz处理速度的多摄像头集成方法，通过结合里程计和集成预测将F1分数提升3个百分点，近车MAE降低30%以上，在真实车辆部署中实现了F1>0.9和侧向MAE<0.18m的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车道检测研究主要面向常规驾驶场景，缺乏对赛车场景（高速、极端道路几何）的探索，因此需要构建专门的赛车车道检测数据集和方法。

Method: 提出多摄像头集成方法，利用惯性测量数据进行预积分以回归道路几何，结合里程计信息，在保持近300Hz处理速度的同时提升检测性能。

Result: 相比BevLaneDet等方法，F1分数提升3个百分点，近车MAE降低30%以上；实际部署中F1>0.9，侧向MAE<0.18m。

Conclusion: 惯性测量和集成预测能显著提升赛车场景下的3D车道检测性能，且所提方法在高处理速度下仍保持高精度，适用于实时赛车自动驾驶系统。

Abstract: Lane boundary detection is a critical component in autonomous driving systems and has been rigorously studied in regular driving scenarios. However, it is less explored in vehicle racing, where the car moves at higher speeds across more extreme road geometries. To study this problem, we introduce a new dataset for 3D lane detection in racing, featuring >$250$k images from multiple camera feeds and inertial measurements taken with a Lexus LC 500 driving on a closed circuit. With this dataset, we compare various approaches to 3D lane detection and propose modifications that permit frames to be processed at rates of almost 300Hz while retaining high predictive performance in the racing application. This facilitates a multi-camera ensemble approach that is validated on hardware. We show that sensing modalities such as inertial measurements can be leveraged for pre-integration to regress road geometries over both cameras and time, yielding improvements in key metrics. Compared to methods such as BevLaneDet, adding odometry and ensemble predictions improves the F1 score by 3 points and reduces near-vehicle mean absolute errors (MAEs) by $>30 \%$. We show F1 scores $>$0.9 and lateral MAEs of $<$0.18m in vehicle deployments.

</details>


### [82] [MonteRET: AI Agent Enhancing Multimodal LLMs with Multi-granularity Knowledge Retrieval for Chest CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14264)
*Yi Lin,Yihao Ding,Elana Benishay,Elefterios Trikantzopoulos,David Nauheim,Hanley Ong,Jiang Bian,Hua Xu,Yuzhe Yang,George Shih,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonteRET是一个区域感知的检索增强框架，用于自动生成胸部CT报告。它整合全局和区域级CT特征，通过预测的医学条件和区域级视觉-语言对齐检索相关知识，并使用知识引导的报告重写代理优化初始报告。在RadGenome-ChestCT（24,128训练/1,564测试）和外部82例CT上评估，相比基线和SOTA方法在报告质量、语义相似度和临床效能上均有提升，召回率改善最显著，放射科住院医师的专家评估也倾向MonteRET。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动胸部CT报告生成的挑战在于需要同时理解整体体积和准确描述局部解剖发现，现有方法难以兼顾全局与局部的细粒度对齐。

Method: 提出MonteRET框架：(1) 整合全局CT特征与区域级解剖表示；(2) 利用预测的医学条件和区域级视觉-语言对齐检索临床相关知识；(3) 通过知识引导的报告重写代理（agent）对初始报告进行精炼。

Result: 在RadGenome-ChestCT测试集（1,564例）和外部数据集（82例）上，MonteRET在报告质量、语义相似度和临床效能上均优于匹配基线和多个SOTA方法，召回率提升最为显著，表明遗漏发现更少。放射科住院医师的专家评估也偏向MonteRET。

Conclusion: 区域感知的检索增强生成框架能有效提升胸部CT报告生成的临床忠实度和完整度，尤其在减少发现遗漏方面表现突出。

Abstract: Automated chest CT report generation remains challenging because clinically faithful reporting requires both whole-volume understanding and accurate description of localized anatomical findings. Here we developed and retrospectively evaluated MonteRET, a region-aware retrieval-enhanced framework for generating chest CT findings sections. MonteRET integrates global CT features with region-level anatomical representations, retrieves clinically relevant knowledge using predicted medical conditions and region-level vision-language alignment, and refines initial reports through a knowledge-guided report rewriting agent. We trained our model on a public cohort with 24,128 CT scans from RadGenome-ChestCT. We evaluated MonteRET on the public RadGenome-ChestCT test set of 1,564 CT scans and an external cohort of 82 CT scans from NewYork-Presbyterian/Weill Cornell Medical Center. MonteRET improved report quality, semantic similarity, and clinical efficacy compared with a matched baseline and several state-of-the-art methods. Gains were most pronounced for recall, suggesting fewer omitted findings. Human expert evaluation by radiology residents also favored MonteRET.

</details>


### [83] [XCT-SAM: Sequential Parameter-Efficient Domain Adaptation of SAM for Industrial XCT Defect Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14287)
*Md Mahedi Hasan,Md Mushfiqur Rahaman,Alan Pachkovskiy,Imtiaz Ahmed,Jeremy Dawson,Srinjoy Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XCT-SAM，一种针对增材制造XCT图像缺陷分割的序列化参数高效微调框架。通过在合金显微结构数据集上先微调Conv-LoRA适配器，再迁移到XCT图像，渐进式缩小域差距。仅训练约415万参数（冻结99%以上模型），在分布外CycleGAN-XCT基准和真实NIST XCT扫描上均优于零样本SAM及其他域适应SAM基线，取得最佳IoU和Dice分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造XCT图像中缺陷分割面临严重的类别不平衡和不同扫描条件间的分布偏移问题。虽然SAM等基础模型提供强大的通用分割先验，但其自然图像预训练难以迁移到AM XCT领域（缺陷表现为细微的非语义微观结构异常），且标注真实XCT数据稀缺，域差距大。

Method: 提出XCT-SAM序列化参数高效适应框架。首先在合金显微结构数据集上微调Conv-LoRA适配器（秩r=2），然后将适应后的模型迁移到XCT图像，逐步缩小域差距。Conv-LoRA适配器向SAM骨干注入卷积空间归纳偏置，仅训练约415万参数，冻结超过99%的模型参数。

Result: 在分布外CycleGAN-XCT基准和真实NIST XCT扫描上，XCT-SAM均一致优于零样本SAM和其他域适应SAM基线，取得最佳总体IoU和Dice分数。

Conclusion: 中间域适应结合参数高效适配器对于工业XCT缺陷分割是有效的策略，XCT-SAM成功弥合了自然图像与AM XCT域之间的巨大差距。

Abstract: Defect segmentation in additive manufacturing (AM) X-ray computed tomography (XCT) images remains challenging due to severe class imbalance and large distribution shifts across scan conditions. Although recent foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) provide strong general-purpose segmentation priors, their natural-image pre-training transfers poorly to the AM XCT domain, where defects appear as subtle non-semantic microstructural anomalies. Moreover, adapting SAM to the AM domain is further limited by the large domain gap and scarcity of labeled real XCT data. We present XCT-SAM, a sequential parameter-efficient adaptation framework for AM XCT defect segmentation. Instead of adapting SAM directly from natural images to XCT data, we first fine-tune Conv-LoRA adapters on an alloy-microstructure dataset and subsequently transfer the adapted model to XCT images, progressively bridging the domain gap. Using Conv-LoRA adapters with rank r=2, the framework injects convolutional spatial inductive bias into SAM's backbone while training approximately 4.15M parameters and keeping over 99% of the model frozen. We evaluate XCT-SAM on out-of-distribution CycleGAN-XCT benchmarks and real-world NIST XCT scans. Across both settings, XCT-SAM consistently outperforms zero-shot SAM and other domain-adapted SAM baselines, achieving the best overall IoU and Dice scores. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of intermediate domain adaptation with parameter-efficient adapters for industrial XCT defect segmentation. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Mahedi-61/XCT-SAM.git

</details>


### [84] [DCVC-MB: Neural B-Frame Video Compression using State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14305)
*Arjun Arora,Calvin-Khang Ta,Carlos Restrepo-Galeano,Kruthi Murali,Naga Akhil E S,Arunkumar Mohananchettiar,Jay Shingala,Tong Shao,Peng Yin,Sean McCarthy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DCVC-Mamba，一种基于状态空间模型的B帧神经网络视频编码框架，采用IBP帧策略、时空融合模型和熵感知跳过机制，在BD-rate指标上相比现有神经视频编码器平均降低8.98%，相比VTM-19.0-LDP和VTM-19.0-RA(Inter-GoP=16)分别提升30.45%和1.81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经视频编码器在B帧编码中性能有限，尤其缺乏高效的双向时域预测机制和编码效率优化方法，作者旨在提出一种更高效的B帧神经视频编码框架。

Method: 1) 采用IBP帧策略实现低延迟B帧编码；2) 基于状态空间模型（SSM）构建时空融合模型进行双向时域预测；3) 设计熵感知跳过机制选择性省略部分潜变量的编码以降低熵编码时间；4) 提出两种推理时策略进一步增强压缩性能。

Result: 相比现有神经视频编码器BD-rate平均降低8.98%；相比VTM-19.0-LDP提升30.45%；相比VTM-19.0-RA(Inter-GoP=16)提升1.81%。

Conclusion: DCVC-Mamba在B帧神经视频编码上显著优于现有神经视频编码器和传统编码标准，推动了神经视频压缩技术的发展。

Abstract: In this paper we propose DCVC-Mamba (DCVC-MB), a neural video codec framework for B-frame coding. Our approach incorporates an IBP frame strategy for low-delay B-frame coding, a spatio-temporal fusion model based on state-space models for bidirectional temporal prediction, and an entropy-aware skipping mechanism that selectively omits coding certain latents to reduce entropy coding times. In addition to our model contributions we also implement two inference-time strategies that enhance compression performance. Experimental evaluation shows that DCVC-MB compares favorably to existing NVCs and traditional codecs. The method demonstrates BD-rate reductions of up to $8.98\%$ on average compared to prior neural video codecs, and improvements of up to $30.45\%$ and $1.81\%$ over the VTM-19.0-LDP and VTM-19.0-RA(Inter-GoP=16) benchmarks, respectively, contributing to advances in neural video compression.

</details>


### [85] [SD-MAR: Multi-image Analytical Reasoning via Synthetic Data and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14333)
*Shiyu Yuan,Sourav Sanjukta Bhabesh,Zhe Wang,Dmitriy Bespalov,Wesley Rose,Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SD-MAR框架，通过构造扰动对比的视觉场景对和GRPO-lite（带BDA的强化学习）训练方法，显著提升VLM在多图像分析推理任务上的性能，Qwen2.5-VL-7B在域内准确率提升36.95%，超越GPT-4.1，同时保持或提升域外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在感知能力上表现强劲，但在需要跨多个视觉状态进行分析推理的任务（如多图比较、变化检测、多步视觉推理）中仍存在局限，而现有基准很少同时要求显式的视觉比较与分析推理，导致这一能力未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 构建SD-MAR数据集：通过可控扰动生成配对的视觉场景，并构造语义变化归因和定量比较两类推理任务；2) 提出GRPO-lite（带BDA的强化学习方法）：去除KL正则化以鼓励更强的策略优化，同时通过向后折扣分配机制将更大信用分配给形成分析结论的后置推理步骤。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B和InternVL3-8B上，GRPO-lite微调在SD-MAR基准上域内准确率提升最高达36.95%；Qwen2.5-VL-7B在SD-MAR基准上超越GPT-4.1；域外泛化在MME、MMMU-Pro、MathVista上波动在1%以内，在MMBench上提升达4%；LLM-as-judge评估显示逻辑连贯性与解释质量持续提升。

Conclusion: SD-MAR框架结合强化学习方法能有效提升VLM的多图像分析推理能力，不仅实现域内大幅提升，还能保持甚至改善域外泛化性能，验证了该方法在真实多模态应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong perceptual abilities but remain limited in tasks requiring analytical reasoning across multiple visual states, such as multi-image comparison, change detection, and multi-step visual inference. These capabilities are critical for real-world multimodal applications where reasoning must be grounded in systematic differences between visual contexts. However, existing benchmarks rarely require both explicit visual comparison and analytical reasoning, leaving this capability underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce SD-MAR (Synthetic Data for Multi-image Analytical Reasoning), a framework for training and evaluating VLMs on multi-image analytical reasoning. SD-MAR constructs paired visual scenarios through controlled perturbations and generates reasoning tasks spanning semantic change attribution and quantitative comparison. We further train VLMs using GRPO-lite with Backward Discounted Allocation (BDA), a reinforcement learning approach that removes KL regularization to encourage stronger policy optimization while allocating greater credit to the later reasoning steps where analytical conclusions are formed. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3-8B show that GRPO-lite fine-tuning on SD-MAR improves in-domain accuracy by up to 36.95%, with Qwen2.5-VL-7B outperforming GPT-4.1 on the SD-MAR benchmark. Importantly, out-of-domain generalization is preserved or improved: performance remains within 1% on MME, MMMU-Pro, and MathVista, while improving by up to 4% on MMBench. LLM-as-judge evaluation further demonstrates consistent improvements in logical coherence and explanation quality across both models.

</details>


### [86] [MixCompress: Mixture of Experts for Variable Rate Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14334)
*Calvin-Khang Ta,Praneet Singh,Tong Shao,Peng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MixCompress框架，通过稀疏结构专业化（MoE+MoD）和条件辅助变换（CAT）解决变比特率图像压缩中的特征纠缠问题，实现单一模型匹配甚至超越单率模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变比特率（VBR）方法共享主干网络导致低速率平滑梯度与高速率纹理细节保留之间存在严重特征纠缠（feature entanglement），使得单一模型逼近不同率失真映射时性能次优。

Method: 1）使用稀疏门控混合专家（MoE）路由缓解梯度冲突；2）引入混合深度（MoD）扩展动态调整模型容量以适应高比特率表示需求；3）设计条件辅助变换（CAT）实现子带能量动态调制，形成分层动态容量扩展框架。

Result: MixCompress不仅匹配独立优化的单率基线，甚至能超越它们，建立了计算高效图像编码的新帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏结构专业化取代密集参数调制，可有效解耦不同率失真目标下的梯度冲突，实现单一模型在整个率失真曲线上接近甚至超越专用模型的最优性能。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) is bottlenecked by the need to store independent models for each rate-distortion operating point. Existing variable bit-rate (VBR) methods aim to reduce this overhead via dense parameter modulation, but forcing a shared backbone to approximate divergent mappings causes severe feature entanglement. Specifically, low-rate smoothing gradients inherently conflict with the preservation of high-frequency textural details, leading to sub-optimal performance. To resolve this, we propose MixCompress, a unified VBR framework based on sparse structural specialization. While sparsely gated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing successfully mitigates gradient conflict, it operates on a fixed computational budget. To address the increased representational demands of higher bit-rates we introduce a Mixture-of-Depths (MoD) extension to dynamically scale model capacity. Combined with Conditional Auxiliary Transforms (CAT) for dynamic sub-band energy modulation, our hierarchical framework effectively dynamically scales capacity. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that MixCompress not only matches individually optimized single-rate baselines but can even surpass them, establishing a new Pareto frontier for computationally efficient image coding.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond scalar losses: calibrating segmentation models via gradient vector field surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14338)
*Laurin Lux,Alexander H. Berger,Moritz Knolle,Daniel Rückert,Johannes C. Paetzold*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对基于区域的损失函数（如Dice损失）导致的模型过自信与校准不良问题的梯度"手术"方法，通过在损失偏导数中添加一个与预测误差线性相关的缩放因子来缓解校准问题，在2D和3D医学分割任务中验证了其有效性且不影响预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于区域的损失函数（如Dice损失）在类别和区域不平衡的分割任务中表现优异，但训练出的模型普遍存在校准不良和过自信预测的问题，这在医学影像应用（如肿瘤切除边界定义）中阻碍了临床采用。

Method: 从梯度视角分析区域损失函数导致过自信的原因，提出对梯度向量场进行"手术"：在损失函数的偏导数中添加一个因子，使得梯度幅度与预测误差呈线性关系缩放。

Result: 在2D和3D医学分割任务上的实证评估表明，该干预方法有效缓解了校准问题，同时在使用任何基于区域的损失函数时均能保持高预测精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的梯度手术是一种简单而有效的干预手段，能够在不牺牲分割精度的前提下显著改善基于区域损失函数的模型校准性能，有望推动其在临床医学影像中的应用。

Abstract: Region-based loss functions, such as the Dice loss, have established themselves as the de facto standard for highly class- and region-imbalanced segmentation tasks. However, models trained using region-based loss functions are notoriously miscalibrated and typically yield over-confident predictions. In medical imaging applications, such as defining tumor resection margins, this miscalibration is hindering clinical adoption. In this work, we outline a novel gradient perspective on this overconfidence and show how it affects region-based loss functions. We propose a "surgery" on the gradient vector field as a simple, yet effective intervention to mitigate calibration issues. This surgery adds a factor to the loss's partial derivative, scaling the gradient's magnitude linearly with the prediction error. In empirical evaluations across 2D and 3D medical segmentation tasks, we demonstrate the effectiveness of this intervention while maintaining high prediction accuracy when used in conjunction with any region-based loss function.

</details>


### [88] [Dynamic Manipulation Hypergraphs for HAR: Beyond Pairwise Relations: Dynamic Manipulation Hypergraphs for Vision-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14350)
*Fatemeh Ziaeetabar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态操作超图框架，通过高阶关系建模（超边）替代传统成对图，在细粒度操作识别任务中显著提升性能，并在EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR和Assembly101上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于图的方法使用成对边（pairwise edges）建模手、物体、工具和支撑面之间的关系，容易将协调的操作事件割裂为不连通的二元关系，无法捕捉多实体间的高阶协同语义。

Method: 构建动态超图框架：每个时间步对多实体编码外观、空间、运动、语义角色特征；利用邻近、接触和运动耦合谓词对超边候选实例化并排序；通过节点-超边和超边-节点的消息传递进行推理，并结合时序注意力捕捉演化结构；输出类无关的超边重要性分数以辨识关键实体配置及时段。

Result: 在EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR上HO-F1较配对图提升6.9个百分点，较静态超图提升4.4个百分点；在Assembly101上分别提升9.5和5.8个百分点；ARCTIC定性分析显示高分超边与接触密集型操作时段高度对应。

Conclusion: 时变高阶关系建模对细粒度操作行为识别具有重要价值，动态超图框架能有效捕获多实体间的协同交互结构，优于传统成对图和静态超图方法。

Abstract: Fine-grained manipulation recognition requires modeling evolving relations among hands, objects, tools, and supporting surfaces. Conventional graph-based methods use pairwise edges that can fragment a coordinated event into disconnected binary relations. We propose a dynamic manipulation hypergraph framework that represents multi-entity configurations as higher-order relational units. At each temporal step, relevant entities are encoded using appearance, spatial, motion, and semantic-role features. Hyperedge candidates are instantiated and ranked using proximity, contact, and motion-coupling predicates. A hypergraph reasoning network performs node-to-hyperedge and hyperedge-to-node message passing, followed by temporal attention over the evolving interaction structure. The framework provides class-agnostic hyperedge-importance scores that identify entity configurations and temporal intervals emphasized by the model without treating them as causal explanations. Quantitative evaluation is conducted on EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR and Assembly101 under an annotation-assisted entity-localization protocol. Video-only and entity-based methods provide contextual comparisons, while a matched pairwise graph and a static hypergraph serve as the principal controlled baselines because they use identical entity inputs and comparable relational settings. The proposed method improves HO-F1 over the matched pairwise graph by 6.9 percentage points on EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR and 9.5 points on Assembly101, and exceeds the static hypergraph by 4.4 and 5.8 points, respectively. Qualitative analysis on ARCTIC further shows correspondence between highly ranked hyperedges and contact-rich manipulation intervals. These results demonstrate the value of time-varying higher-order relational modeling for fine-grained manipulation activity recognition.

</details>


### [89] [$K$-NeAS: Scalable Multi-Material CT Reconstruction Using Neural SDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14415)
*Daksh K. Shah,Emmanouil Nikolakakis,Razvan V. Marinescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出K-NeAS，一种统一可扩展的架构，用于自动多材料表面重建。它用共享潜变量骨干网络替代独立材料网络，引入可微分的K材料顺序软选择器，通过高斯混合模型自动衰减界，并设计辅助浮动损失以减少几何幻觉。在四个CBCT数据集上，K-NeAS在K=3时达到33.28 dB 3D PSNR，比单材料基线提升1.88 dB，在稀疏采样下最高提升1.17 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式场景表示方法在稀疏CT重建中受限于手动调参的衰减界和刚性双材料约束，无法扩展到任意数量的组织材料，且存在几何幻觉问题。

Method: 1) 共享潜变量骨干网络替代独立材料网络；2) 可微分的K材料顺序软选择器建模任意数量重叠组织；3) 高斯混合模型自动衰减界；4) 调度辅助浮动损失减少稀疏视图下的几何幻觉。

Result: 在四个CBCT数据集上，K=3时腹部区域3D PSNR达33.28 dB，较单材料基线提升1.88 dB；5视图和10视图稀疏条件下3D PSNR最高提升1.17 dB。

Conclusion: K-NeAS成功扩展到任意材料数量，在复杂多组织区域实现优越的3D体积保真度，并在稀疏采样条件下具有更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Computed Tomography (CT) carries significant ionizing radiation risks, driving the need for sparse-view reconstruction. Implicit scene representations (ISRs) address this by recovering continuous volumetric attenuation fields directly from sparse projections, and recent geometry-aware extensions jointly model surface geometry alongside attenuation to improve fidelity and enable clean tissue segmentation without manual thresholding. However, these methods remain limited by manually tuned attenuation bounds and rigid two-material constraints. This paper proposes $K$-NeAS, a unified and scalable architecture for automated, multi-material surface reconstruction. We replace independent material networks with a shared latent backbone and introduce a fully differentiable $K$-material sequential soft selector to model an arbitrary number of overlapping tissues. To eliminate manual tuning, we automate attenuation bounding using a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) and implement a scheduled auxiliary floater loss to mitigate geometric hallucinations common under extreme sparsity. Evaluated across four clinical Cone-Beam CT (CBCT) datasets, $K$-NeAS successfully scales to arbitrary material counts, achieving superior 3D volumetric fidelity at $K=3$ materials on complex multi-tissue regions such as the Abdomen ($33.28\text{ dB}$ 3D PSNR vs. $31.40\text{ dB}$ single-material NeAS baseline, a $+1.88\text{ dB}$ improvement). Furthermore, our model exhibits enhanced robustness under sparse-sampling conditions, outperforming baseline 3D PSNR by up to $1.17\text{ dB}$ under 5- and 10-view constraints.

</details>


### [90] [Emergent Region-Level Facial Correspondence in Frozen Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14423)
*Izaldein Al-Zyoud,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.CV
